### Seedance 2.0 Realistic Female Dance Video Prompt

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)

> A detailed prompt for generating a 15-second vertical video of a woman dancing using Seedance 2.0. It specifies camera movements, choreography details, and maintains character consistency.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
15 seconds, 9:16 aspect ratio, realistic female TikTok dance short video. One continuous shot, maintaining the reference character's appearance, clothing, and scene. Hand movements do not go above the head throughout. Left and right refer to the dancer's own left and right.

Overall performance: Two distinct continuous dance routines, connected by 'right hand grabs/pulls lens close to face, left hand throws open pulling lens away'. The character continues jumping within the same dance area, with shoulders, hips, and arms synchronized. Movements have a contrast between expansive and quick retraction, each segment directly leading into the next.

[0-3s | First Routine: Hip Support Extension & Retraction]
CAMERA POV: Frontal medium shot, from top of head to above knees, fully presenting shoulders, waist, hips, and arms.

Already dancing at the start. Left hand supports hip, right palm extends towards the lens then retracts to front of shoulder, coordinating with body tilting right and swinging back left; knees are elastic, shoulder line tilts as hips change direction. First extension is expansive, second retraction is quick, body starts next direction change during arm retraction. Head sways lightly with body, initially relaxed looking at lens, showing a slight smile when retracting hand the second time.

[3-6s | Crossed Sinking, Palm Push Direction Change]
Right hand naturally falls to front of abdomen, left hand leaves side of waist, both forearms cross in front of waist/abdomen, knees bend slightly, body sinks down; immediately after, arms open, body rises back to left side.

Left palm touches chest, right palm pushes from chest towards front-right, shoulders/chest turn into slight oblique side with push; return to upright when retracting palm. Then push forward again once more, hips change direction simultaneously. Palms always have clear orientation, push and retract form one complete short phrase.

The last push naturally extends into reaching towards the lens.

[6-8s | Grab Pull Close to Face, Throw Hand Away Pull Lens Back]
Right hand reaches towards lens, five fingers clearly close into a loose grab; immediately after, right elbow sharply pulls back behind, fist pulls along side of face back to front of right shoulder, shoulders/chest pulled back accordingly.

CAMERA POV: Maintain distance when reaching/grabbing; the moment right elbow starts pulling back, lens synchronously rapidly zooms into facial close-up, decelerating in front of face. Lens movement completes simultaneously with this pull.

At the moment of closeness to face, she slightly turns head, eyes accurately catch the lens, corners of mouth lift up. Body rhythm continues, right hand lowers along chest, left hand immediately throws open from chest towards left side, hands and shoulders together bring out this throw.

CAMERA POV: The moment left hand throws open, rapidly pull back, restore medium shot from top of head to above knees, character remains centered throughout.

[8-11s | Entering Second Routine During Pull Back: Alternating Arms at Shoulder Side]
While lens is pulling back, thrown-out left arm has already bent elbow to retract, right arm simultaneously lifts up, two loosely gripped hands arrive below shoulder level.

Shoulders alternate pushing forward left and right, bent elbows respond front-to-back with shoulders; hips coordinate weight shifting left and right, knees continue light bouncing. After completing one set of left-right alternation, hands drop to cross in front of waist, then immediately open to sides, bringing out a clear sink and rise. Movements are coherent, crossing becomes momentum storage for next expansion.

Expression naturally relaxes from the smile at face-close moment, head lightly tilts with shoulder line, looking straight at lens again when arms open.

[11-15s | Pointing Near Face, Low Position Expansion]\nFollowing arms opening, right hand retracts to side of face forming a clear single finger pointing up shape, left forearm stays in front of waist/abdomen. Left foot takes small step landing on left side bearing weight, hips send left, shoulder line tilts opposite; when body changes direction, left forearm expands sideways, right hand briefly maintains face-side shape.

Right hand then drops along chest, crosses with left forearm in front of waist/abdomen, knees bend slightly; immediately uncross, elbows approach waist sides, forearms expand to both sides, coordinating with two consecutive left-right weight shifts. Head sways lightly with direction change, finally turning face to look at lens, smile appearing again.

When the frame ends, still completing the last lateral move and rebound, retaining dance momentum.
```

### Seedance 2.0 Mini Ancient Xianxia Video Prompt

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)

> A detailed prompt for generating a 15-second cinematic video in the ancient Chinese Xianxia style using Seedance 2.0 Mini, featuring character consistency and narrative structure.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Seedance 2.0 Mini | Ancient Xianxia "The Only One Who Failed"

Model: Seedance 2.0 Mini
Duration: Strictly 15 seconds
Aspect Ratio: 16:9 Landscape
Structure: Three continuous, clear shots with different narrative functions
I. Prompt | Overall Style and Creative Direction
Cinematic realistic texture, pure ancient Chinese Xianxia aesthetics.
Core elements:
Psychological suspense
Restrained social satire
Precise micro-acting
High-level martial arts scene scheduling
Adopt:
Natural volumetric lighting
Realistic skin texture
Delicate silk movement
Cinematic depth of field
Creative Background
Inspired by real-world controversies regarding tampered exam scores, but the plot is completely reconstructed as a fictional ancient sect sword trial.
II. Core Plot and Twist
Everyone assumes the junior sister who failed lacks ability, but the problem lies with the exam itself.
Others can buy fake qualifications to pass, while she chooses to face her true level.
Three-layer Narrative Progression
Layer 1: Public Humiliation
150 disciples participate in the sword trial; only the junior sister fails.
Layer 2: Institutional Reversal
The examiner offers to modify results for spirit stones, implying others' qualifications were also tampered with.
Layer 3: Active Choice
The junior sister refuses to buy qualification, abandoning false certificates to continue practicing swords.
Core Emotions
First half: Humiliation, confusion, suppression
Middle: Shock, cognitive reversal
Second half: Clarity, restraint, re-determination
III. Character and Asset Locking
@Image1 | Sword Immortal Senior Sister
Same Senior Sister:
25-30 yo East Asian female
Pale oval face
Dark almond eyes
Black long hair half-up with white jade hairpin
Tall and slender figure
Same white embroidered silk Hanfu
Semi-transparent layered wide sleeves
Silver waistband
Jade pendant
White cloth boots
Carrying one silver longsword
@Image2 | Junior Sister
Same Junior Sister:
20-25 yo East Asian female
Round lively face
Black braided hair
Petite figure
Same blue-green linen Hanfu
Dark belt
Wooden hairpin
Black cloth shoes
Carrying one dark steel sword
Other Characters
Elder Examiner
Hosts the sect sword trial, announces results, and proposes modifying qualifications.
Passed Disciples
Several disciples stand on one side holding identical red-stamped qualification documents.
Other characters serve only necessary narrative functions.
IV. Environment DNA and Spatial Planning
All newly uploaded background/location reference images determine the physical world.
Scene Locking
Preserve recognizable features from references:
Terrain
Main architectural spatial relationships
Spatial scale
Materials
Weather
Vegetation
Lighting
Air depth
Naturally place a simple ancient sect assessment desk based on existing space.
Do not reset the world arbitrarily.
Living Background Principle
Environment remains alive:
Breeze flows naturally
Clouds change continuously
Vegetation sways naturally
Distant figures maintain subtle activity
Sleeves move with character actions and natural wind
Spatial ambient sound persists
Narrative Neutrality
Background remains narratively neutral.
Must not actively influence:
Exam results
Examiner behavior
Character choices
Plot twists
V. 15-Second Complete Storyboard Structure
0-5s | Shot 1: Wide Shot | Public Humiliation, Establish Suspense
Camera Design
Slow push-in from medium-long shot of the sword trial site.
Space and Character Relations
An elder examiner stands by the assessment desk.
Several passed disciples stand on one side holding red-stamped qualification documents.
The junior sister stands alone on the other side.
The senior sister stands diagonally behind her.
Action and Dialogue
The examiner announces publicly:
[Examiner] "Out of 150 people, only you failed."
Surrounding disciples show slight murmurs and suppressed laughter.
The junior sister does not defend herself, slowly lowering her eyes.
The senior sister remains calm, not immediately stepping forward.
Visual Contrast
Form distinct contrast through:
Red-stamped qualification documents in other disciples' hands
The junior sister's empty hands
Shot 1 Narrative Goal
Must make viewers naturally question:
Why did only she fail?
5-10s | Shot 2: Medium Close-Up | From Failure to Institutional Reversal
Continuity Requirements
Maintain continuity of:
Character identities
Props
Spatial positions
Examiner's covert actions
Seeing attention shift, the examiner leans slightly down.
He retrieves an unstamped qualification document from under the desk, quietly pushing it toward the junior sister.
Dialogue
Examiner whispers:
[Examiner] "Ten spirit stones, I'll fix it for you."
The junior sister looks up sharply.
Junior Sister's Reaction
She doesn't get angry immediately.
First looks at the red-stamped documents in others' hands, then asks slowly:
[Junior Sister] "Did they cheat too?"
The examiner doesn't answer.
Only gives a very light, unmistakable nod.
Camera and Performance
Camera stays on the junior sister's face.
Her true shock isn't failing the exam.
It's realizing those enviable scores might not be earned by skill.
Information Reversal Principle
At this point:
No flashbacks needed
No explanation of cheating process needed
The same red-stamped documents, examiner's hint, and that nod are enough to complete the information reversal.
10-15s | Shot 3: Character Close-Up | Active Choice, Emotional Landing
Key Action 1: Refusing Qualification
The junior sister's hand slowly reaches for the qualification document on the desk.
Just before fingertips touch the paper, she stops.
Then, she gently pushes the document back toward the examiner.
Performance Restrictions
No:
Throwing things
Drawing sword threats
Shouting accusations
Core Dialogue
Looking at the examiner, she only asks:
[Junior Sister] "When demons come, who will fix it for me?"
The examiner freezes, speechless for the first time.
Key Action 2: Senior Sister Steps In
The senior sister steps forward half a step from behind the junior sister, coming to her side.
The senior sister does NOT:
Declare she is truly first
Demand a passing rank for her
Only lightly touches the scabbard at the junior sister's waist with her fingertip.
Calmly says:
[Senior Sister] "Let's go, practice swords."
Junior Sister's Emotional Change
The junior sister looks up at her.
Her tense expression gradually relaxes.
She turns and leaves the assessment desk with the senior sister.
Final Image and Emotional Resonance
Camera retains a brief final emotional resonance:
The examiner still stands in place
The purchasable qualification document on the desk remains unclaimed
Final Narrative Landing
She didn't suddenly pass the exam, nor receive hidden rewards.
She is still the one who failed the sword trial.
But she finally knows what to do next.
VI. Seedance 2.0 Mini Execution Requirements
Basic Specs
Must generate a 15-second Seedance 2.0 Mini video
Strict total duration: 15 seconds
Aspect ratio: 16:9 landscape
Three continuous, clear shots with different narrative functions
Model Generation Focus
Emphasize Seedance 2.0 Mini's:
Dual protagonist identity consistency
Clear visual hierarchy
Few but precise action nodes
Stable camera movement
Realistic character micro-expressions
Synchronized dialogue and spatial audio
VII. Dialogue and Lip Sync
Dialogue uses natural Mandarin.
All characters must match lip movements precisely when speaking.
Speaker Requirements
When examiner speaks, only examiner lip-syncs
When junior sister speaks, only junior sister lip-syncs
When senior sister speaks, only senior sister lip-syncs
For non-dialogue moments, express emotion via:
Eye contact
Pauses
Hand gestures
Spatial blocking
VIII. Character, Prop, and Space Continuity
Maintain consistency of:
Characters
Face shape
Hairstyle
Clothing
Body proportions
Weapons
Senior Sister's single silver longsword
Junior Sister's single dark steel sword
Props
Red-stamped qualification document
Unstamped qualification document
Ancient sect assessment desk
Space
Examiner's position
Other disciples' positions
Relative positions of senior and junior sisters
Main building and terrain relations
IX. Sound and Cinematic Quality
Sound Design
Ensure synchronization of:
Mandarin dialogue
Paper sliding sounds
Fabric friction sounds
Soft scabbard taps
Natural spatial ambience
Performance and Photography
Restrained action count
Highlight micro-expressions
Stable camera movement
Clear visual hierarchy
Realistic character relations and spatial blocking
Background Requirements
Background remains naturally alive.
But has no autonomous causal relationship with exam cheating or character choices.
X. Strict Prohibitions
No subtitles generated
Do not require model to generate readable exam score text
Do not arbitrarily reset reference environment
No actions unrelated to plot
Do not use environmental events to drive exam fraud twist
Do not change characters, weapons,
```

### Seedance 2.0 Kitchen Vlog Prompt with Character Consistency

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)

> A detailed prompt for generating a 10-second vertical kitchen vlog video using Seedance 2.0. It specifies character consistency across shots, prop usage (food tongs/spatula), lighting conditions, and camera movements to create a realistic home-style short video.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Character:
Face: Appearance, features, hairstyle, hair color, and makeup strictly follow the headshot reference image.
Clothing and Body: Outfit, accessories, and body proportions strictly follow the cream-white butterfly dress reference image (off-shoulder cream dress + butterfly hair accessory); Shot 2 back view matches front view outfit.
Consistency: Same person across 4 shots; face and clothing remain consistent throughout, no changes after cuts; mirror reflection in Shot 2 is also the same person and outfit.

Props:
Shot 1: A pair of black kitchen food tongs.
Shot 4: A black spatula.

Scene:
A home kitchen connected to a living room through a doorway.

Kitchen: White marble-textured tile walls, black stove with a wok, condiment bottles, knife block on counter; white kitchen door, red lanterns hanging near ceiling.
Living Room: Light wood floor, full-length mirror behind door, black cat on floor.

Lighting:
Indoor natural daylight, soft and even, cool white-gray tone, clean and transparent.

Storyboard
Shot 1 | 0.00–3.27s | Wide-angle close-up, interacting with camera holding food tongs

Camera position: Phone very close to subject, slightly high angle, wide lens, framing face and upper body, background shows tile wall, white door, black wok on stove.
0.00–0.60s: Subject looks down with slight smile; left hand holds black food tongs at bottom-left of frame.
0.60–1.00s: Subject lifts head to look at camera, camera moves slightly closer.
1.00–1.60s: Subject looks at camera, corners of mouth rise, food tongs move to left edge of frame.
1.60–2.00s: Subject extends food tongs toward camera, tong heads appear in left foreground with bokeh effect.
2.00–2.60s: Subject clicks food tongs open/closed several times in front of camera, eyes squint slightly, playful smile.
2.60–3.27s: Food tongs retract to left side, subject smiles at camera, elegant and composed movement. 0.3s crossfade transition at 3.27s.

Shot 2 | 3.27–4.70s | Full-length mirror scene, back to camera then turning around

Camera position: Behind subject slightly right, eye-level, medium shot. Left half shows frontal reflection in mirror, right half shows subject's back; black cat on wood floor.
3.27–4.00s: Subject stands with back to camera facing mirror, one hand on hip, other touching mirror edge; reflection looking at herself.
4.00–4.20s: Subject turns head over shoulder to look at camera.
4.20–4.70s: Subject faces camera directly with calm expression; reflection turns to profile, elegant movement. 0.3s crossfade transition at 4.70s.

Shot 3 | 4.70–5.50s | High-angle overhead shot standing by stove

Camera position: From high corner of kitchen looking down full-body, wide lens showing black stove, wok, condiments, white door.
4.70–5.50s: Subject stands by stove with hands behind back, looks up smiling at camera, body sways slightly, elegant movement. 0.3s crossfade transition at 5.50s.

Shot 4 | 5.50–10.00s | Holding spatula, close-up facial expressions

Camera position: Phone close to subject, slightly high angle, wide lens framing chest-up. Background shows white door, handle, marble tiles, black wok, red lanterns.
5.50–6.00s: Subject holds black spatula at shoulder height with head up, smiling at camera.
6.00–8.00s: Subject says line 'Is this spatula easy to use?' while smiling.
8.00–8.40s: Subject raises eyebrows looking at camera, corners of mouth naturally rise.
8.40–10.00s: Subject maintains warm relaxed smile looking at camera, spatula held beside shoulder, smile persists until end freeze-frame. Transitions smooth and continuous, no frame skips or sudden pose changes.

Style:
TikTok/Xiaohongshu home vlog style, phone wide-angle original camera quality, beauty filter/skin smoothing applied, fair translucent skin. Natural daylight, cool white tone, photorealistic, HD, lively natural expressions; overall elegant and composed, slow fluid movements, not exaggerated or rushed.

Audio:
Continuous upbeat music (ukulele/accordion bright instrumental), crossfades at cuts without interruption. In Shot 4, clear natural voice saying 'Is this spatula easy to use?', music lowered but not overpowering voice. No other human voices, screams, laughs, or strange sounds except this line.

Parameters:
9:16 vertical screen, 10.00 seconds, 30fps, photorealistic, 4 shots.

Negative Prompt: Face swap, clothing change, character appearance change after cuts, mirror reflection inconsistent with actual person, reflection becoming different person, distorted features, frowning, sad face, pursed lips, deformed fingers, deformed food tongs/spatula, extra arms, severe wide-angle distortion, flickering, low resolution, watermarks, subtitles, frame skipping, sudden motion jumps, abrupt pose changes, strange human voices, screams, unexplained laughter
```

### Dancing Anthropomorphic Cat Video

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A detailed prompt for generating a photorealistic video of an anthropomorphic cat dancing in a shirt and trousers, emphasizing anatomical stability and rhythmic movement.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
**DURATION:** 8 seconds  
**FORMAT:** Vertical 9:16, cinematic photorealism  
**REFERENCE:** Use the uploaded cat image as the exact first-frame and character reference.

**CHARACTER CONSISTENCY:** The exact same orange-and-cream tabby cat wearing the same pink casual button-up shirt and beige trousers. Preserve its original face, eye shape, ears, fur markings, body proportions, outfit, and tail throughout the entire video.

**MUSIC:** Cool, rhythmic contemporary dance-pop instrumental with a deep, groovy bass, crisp percussion, and stylish club beats. Music begins immediately. Dance movements must match the beat.

**DANCE CHOREOGRAPHY:**

**0–2 seconds:** The cat starts in a relaxed, confident standing pose. It performs a smooth shoulder roll, subtle chest isolation, and controlled side-to-side hip movement while maintaining eye contact with the camera.

**2–4 seconds:** The cat executes clean, stylish footwork, stepping right and left while moving its arms rhythmically across the torso. Natural wrist movements, relaxed elbows, and controlled upper-body grooves.

**4–6 seconds:** The cat performs a smooth body wave, a confident two-step, and coordinated arm sweeps. Its torso and hips move independently in a physically coherent, human-like dance style.

**6–8 seconds:** The cat executes two sharp beat-matched steps, a smooth shoulder pop, and finishes with a cool, confident pose facing the camera.

**CRITICAL HEAD AND NECK CONTROL:** The cat's head remains anatomically stable and naturally connected to its neck. Only subtle, realistic head tilts and small beat-synchronized movements are allowed. The head must NEVER rotate 360 degrees, flip backward, twist unnaturally, detach, or rotate independently of the body. Keep the eyes, ears, muzzle, and facial structure consistent in every frame.

**REALISM LOCK:** Natural weight transfer, grounded feet, believable balance, smooth transitions between dance moves, realistic fur and fabric physics, consistent shadows, continuous motion, and stable anatomy. One uninterrupted shot with a steady full-body camera and no sudden camera movements.

**NEGATIVE CONSTRAINTS:** No cartoon movement, no childish dancing, no exaggerated facial expressions, no rubber-like limbs, no unnatural spine bending, no neck twisting, no head spinning, no extra limbs, no face morphing, no outfit changes, no floating, no flickering, no sudden pose jumps, no artificial-looking motion.

**FINAL RESULT:** A stylish, confident, adult-inspired dance performance by the same photorealistic anthropomorphic cat, with smooth choreography, natural head movement, and convincing physical motion.
```

### Seedance 2.5 DV Home Video Prompt

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)

> A highly detailed prompt for generating a realistic early 2000s home DV video featuring a young East Asian woman in a bedroom on a hot summer afternoon. Includes specific instructions for character consistency, action timeline, camera stability, and audio environment.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Image 1 is the first frame of the video; the scene starts from Image 1, with scene, composition, lighting, clothing, props, and their positions all based on Image 1.
Image 2 is the character reference, used only to lock in the person's face, facial proportions, face shape, and hair color. Do not use the gray background, clothing, or three-view layout from Image 2.

Create a 30-second, hyper-realistic early 2000s home DV recording capturing a young East Asian woman spending a muggy summer afternoon in an old residential apartment bedroom. Pure observational perspective, no dialogue, no narrative arc, just the rhythm of daily life.

Character
Appearance matches Image 2; clothing and hairstyle match Image 1: bare-faced, loose bun on top of head, light blue loose T-shirt, white cotton shorts, barefoot. Maintain consistent face, clothing, hairstyle, and body shape throughout.

Action Timeline (each action performed once, no repetition)
00:00–00:06 — She lies half-reclined against a pillow, slowly fanning herself with the fan from Image 1 in her right hand, staring at the ceiling blankly, occasionally blinking.
00:06–00:11 — She stops fanning, places the fan on the bamboo mat beside her, pushes up to sit on the bed, and turns toward the fan on the bedside stool.
00:11–00:16 — She picks up the soda bottle from the stool, takes a sip through the straw, then puts the bottle back in its original spot.
00:16–00:22 — She leans close to the front of the fan, opens her mouth, and drags out a long "Ah—" into the blades; the sound trembles against the spinning blades, stray hairs blown by the wind. Keep a safe distance between face and blades, do not touch the fan.
00:22–00:26 — She laughs at herself, briefly glances back at the camera with a shy smile, saying nothing.
00:26–00:30 — She falls backward onto the pillow, covers her face with the fan, and stays still. The fan continues to spin, then cut to black.

Consistency
Positions of fan, soda bottle, fan unit, and stool remain constant; no objects appear or disappear spontaneously. Only one fan and one soda bottle in the frame; fan style identical to Image 1. Fan orientation fixed, no oscillation; blade shape stable. Bamboo curtain light/shadow stripes stable. Actions proceed in real-time pace, unhurried.

Cinematography & Quality
Fixed camera angle matching Image 1; no movement, zoom, or pan throughout. Light source is only natural afternoon light through bamboo curtains, slightly overexposed. DV noise, slight color shift, vignette, occasional autofocus breathing. Orange date stamp "2003 08 02" in bottom right corner remains fixed throughout.

Audio
Ambient sounds dominate: hum of fan, soft rustle of fanning, cicadas outside window, distant bicycle bell, creaking of bamboo mat under pressure, sound of drinking soda through straw. Only human voices are the trembling "Ah—" directed at the fan and a light laugh. No background music.

Prohibited
No subtitles, no soundtrack, no slow motion, no cinematic color grading, no camera moves, no shallow depth of field, no gray studio background, no text or brand logos (except date stamp). 30s, 720p
```

It features a young Asian woman in a gym who appears disciplined but ultimately reveals she is eating fried chicken and milk tea, with specific instructions for camera movement, lighting, and negative prompts.

```
15-second ultra-realistic funny short video, 9:16 vertical screen, mobile phone original camera documentary texture, real gym environment, soft indoor top light, throughout the process it looks like a friend casually following along, slight natural shaking, very strong lifestyle feel.

[Character Setting]
High-looking young Asian girl, natural light makeup, delicate and relaxed facial features, well-proportioned figure, gentle and cool temperament, real passerby feel, skin retains natural texture and subtle sweat beads, no influencer smoothing, no exaggerated plastic surgery face, natural body proportions. Face and body completely consistent with reference image.

Shot 1 | 0-4 seconds Illusion of Discipline
Bright and clean gym, background has a few people training and equipment. Girl seriously doing standard squats in free training area, movements smooth and stable. Camera follows from right rear handheld, first slowly retreating to keep full body in frame, then slightly rising as she stands up, like a friend walking and filming, picture has slight breathing feel and natural shaking, creating illusion that she is very disciplined and hardworking.

Shot 2 | 4-8 seconds Misleading Continued Training
Hard cut to side follow shot, girl standing lifting small dumbbells doing curl training, slightly sweating, seriously exerting force, expression calm and focused. Camera close to her front side following, first slight lateral movement, then slowly pushing in to upper body, background equipment naturally sweeping past, audience thinks this is a disciplined fitness video.

Girl suddenly puts down dumbbells, expression changes from focused to relaxed, takes a long breath. Camera continues handheld following her to training bench. She sits down, takes out fried chicken and iced milk tea from carry bag, actions natural and skilled, tears open packaging bag. Camera directly pushes to close-up of face and fried chicken/milk tea in same frame. Girl calmly and naturally bites fried chicken, then drinks milk tea, looks at camera, says in casual tone: "Eat full to have strength to lose weight." After speaking, expression is calm, like saying something normal. Ending stays on her calm eating contrast face, handheld camera has extremely slight forward-backward shaking, enhancing friend's sneaky filming feel.

[Picture Texture]
Ultra-realistic live action, original camera HD quality, real skin texture, pores, sweat beads and natural skin color, slight environmental noise in gym, natural indoor top light, do not use movie-grade exquisite lighting. [Sound Effects]
Gym distant equipment collision sounds, slight footsteps, girl's training breathing sounds, packaging bag tearing sound, fried chicken chewing sound, milk tea straw sound. Dialogue clear and natural, do not use exaggerated dubbing feel. Add short light funny music point after dialogue ends.

[Negative Prompts]
Deformed fingers, twisted limbs, extra fingers, missing fingers, face collapse, misaligned facial features, AI fake face, plastic skin, excessive beauty filter, silicone face, exaggerated contact lenses, deformed body, disproportionate, abnormal head-body ratio, floating objects, object clipping through, picture flickering, object drifting, cartoon rendering, 3D animation, hand-drawn, illustration, screen subtitles, text, flower text, watermark, logo, border, strong filter, soft light smoothing, stabilizer smooth movement, fixed camera position, stiff posing, stage lighting, pale fake light, excessive lighting, extra characters, messy distracting clutter, exaggerated performance, awkward acting, affected expressions
```

### Futuristic Driving POV 2050

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A prompt for a 10-second ultra-photorealistic video showing a woman driving in 2050 with flying vehicles overhead, including detailed character and scene descriptions.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
TITLE: POV — DRIVING IN 2050

Create a 10-second ultra-photorealistic futuristic video showing a beautiful young woman casually driving along a highway in the year 2050, while hundreds of autonomous flying vehicles travel overhead in perfectly organized aerial traffic lanes.

MAIN CHARACTER — STRICT IDENTITY CONSISTENCY

A beautiful 23-year-old woman with long, straight pastel-pink hair, delicate facial features, realistic fair skin with visible natural texture, soft pink makeup, and subtle glossy lips.

She wears:
A fitted blue denim sleeveless top.
A futuristic metallic silver choker necklace.
Bright blue statement earrings.
A black seatbelt across her chest.

Maintain her exact face, hairstyle, outfit, proportions, and accessories throughout.

SCENE 1 — 0:00–0:02 | INSIDE THE CAR
Start with a fixed dashboard-mounted smartphone camera facing the woman from the passenger side.

She sits behind the steering wheel of a modern luxury vehicle, driving casually.

Warm golden-hour sunlight streams through the side window, illuminating her pink hair and face.

She looks ahead at the road, then briefly glances toward the camera with a subtle, amused smile.

The vehicle interior is realistic, with black leather seats, a dark roof, and natural windshield reflections.

SCENE 2 — 0:02–0:04 | THE FUTURISTIC HIGHWAY REVEAL

Hard cut to a spectacular elevated wide-angle view of a massive multilane highway at sunset.
A dark luxury SUV drives along the nearly empty highway.

Above the highway, hundreds of futuristic autonomous flying vehicles hover and travel in tightly organized aerial traffic lanes.

The flying vehicles have compact aerodynamic bodies, metallic silver and dark-gray surfaces, and advanced concealed propulsion systems.

They move smoothly in synchronized formations, maintaining precise distances from one another.

Some fly directly above the highway while others stretch toward the horizon, creating a dense futuristic transportation network.

Golden sunlight reflects off their metallic surfaces.

SCENE 3 — 0:04–0:06 | BACK TO THE DRIVER

Hard cut back to the same dashboard camera angle.

The pink-haired woman continues driving completely unfazed by the extraordinary traffic system outside.
She rests one hand naturally on the steering wheel and looks toward the windshield with a playful, slightly impressed expression.

Sunlight shifts across her face as the vehicle moves.

Her facial movements are subtle, natural, and believable.

SCENE 4 — 0:06–0:08 | MASSIVE AERIAL TRAFFIC

Cut to a dramatic elevated tracking shot following the dark SUV along the highway.

The camera reveals an enormous formation of flying vehicles stretching across the sky.

Several vehicles glide directly above the camera while hundreds more move across multiple aerial levels.
The futuristic traffic looks technologically advanced but physically believable.

Below, the dark SUV continues along the sunlit road, appearing calm amidst the chaos above.
```

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A detailed prompt for a cinematic luxury editorial video set in Manhattan, involving a mix-up with coffee cups leading to a conversation, with character reference instructions.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
The Wrong Coffee — Midtown Manhattan, New York

Duration: 30 seconds | Aspect Ratio: 16:9 | Style: Cinematic luxury editorial

Character References: Use the two supplied images strictly as character references, not starting frames. The man, Alex, is a confident, successful Manhattan finance executive. Preserve his exact face, dark tailored suit, tie, and hairstyle. The woman, Allisa, is the second character; preserve her exact identity, hairstyle, and elegant fitted outfit.

Visual Continuity: Warm, slightly vintage editorial photography with amber highlights, muted contrast, subtle film grain, and realistic skin texture. Consistent color across every scene. No artificial glow or plastic skin.

Environmental Accuracy: All visible signage, menus, names, and street text must be correctly spelled and legible. No gibberish.

Scene 1 — Morning Coffee (0–4s)

Inside an upscale Midtown Manhattan coffee shop with polished stone counters, sophisticated interiors, and busy professionals. Alex approaches the counter, composed and confident. No coffee cups are visible on the counter.

Camera positioned behind Alex, looking toward the counter. A beautiful barista in her very early twenties, with striking model-like features and a naturally charismatic presence, turns slightly toward the off-camera coffee preparation area.

Alex, casually: "Double espresso, please. My name is Alex."

Barista, friendly: "Coming right up."

Display exactly "Midtown Manhattan, New York" in the lower third, horizontally centered at the bottom of the frame, Poppins Semi-Bold 600, white (#FFFFFF), on a rounded charcoal-slate (#17212B) background at 60% opacity. No extra characters.

Scene 2 — The Barista (4–7s)

Camera positioned behind Alex, but a different POV from Scene 1, looking toward the counter.

Barista, calling clearly: "Double espresso for Alex!"

Alex remains absorbed in his phone. No coffee cups are shown in this shot.

Scene 3 — The Wrong Cup (7–11s)

Two takeaway coffees are placed side by side on the pickup counter by an unseen employee. One cup is clearly labeled ALEX, the other ALYSSA.

Still looking at his phone, Alex reaches with his free hand and mistakenly picks up only the cup labeled ALYSSA. He walks away carrying it, unaware of the mistake. His actual coffee, labeled ALEX, remains on the counter.

Scene 4 — The Sidewalk (11–14s)

Outside, Alex walks briskly along a busy Midtown Manhattan sidewalk, carrying ALYSSA's coffee in one hand while continuing to look at his phone in the other.

Behind him, ALYSSA exits the same café that Alex just came out of, holding his coffee and spots him ahead.

Scene 5 — The Encounter (14–19s)

ALYSSA catches up to Alex on the sidewalk.

ALYSSA, lightly amused: "I think you grabbed my coffee."

Alex looks up from his phone, stops, and glances at the cup.

Alex, looking at his cup, and then ALYSSA, with an apologetic smile: "Oh, you're right. Sorry about that."

They naturally exchange cups. Alex puts his phone away.

Scene 6 — Same Direction (19–24s)

Alex gestures down the sidewalk.

Alex, casually: "Which way are you headed?"

ALYSSA, pointing ahead: "Same way as you, apparently."

Alex, smiling: "Well, that's convenient."

They share a laugh and begin walking together, talking comfortably. A fixed camera captures them moving away down the sidewalk until they exit the frame.

Scene 7 — An Unexpected Connection (24–30s)

Hard cut to a fixed camera position farther down the same Midtown block. Alex and ALYSSA walk side by side, coffees in hand, enjoying an animated conversation.

Allisa, playfully: "So, is this your usual morning routine?"

Alex, amused: "Not usually this interesting."

Allisa laughs naturally, completing her laugh without interruption. They continue walking and talking, moving toward the right side of the frame.

Both characters fully exit the frame to the right. Hold the empty sidewalk for the final 0.5 seconds before ending.

Direction: Natural conversational dialogue, accurate lip-sync, subtle emotional expressions, and realistic physical interactions. Alex keeps his phone in one hand throughout Scenes 2–4 and picks up only Allisa's coffee with his free hand. The ALEX cup stays behind until Allisa retrieves it. Maintain exact cup ownership and continuity. Varied cinematic tracking and close-ups, with fixed camera positions for the walking exits. Clean instantaneous hard cuts. Allow dialogue, reactions, and laughter to finish naturally. No slow motion, morphing, subtitles, or music.
```

### Grandma Leopard Gangster Comedy

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A complex prompt for a comedic video involving a grandma, a leopard, and a gangster demanding rent, with strict identity locks and prop references.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
REFERENCES: Grandma = 95–100-year-old tiny hunched Korean grandmother (exact face, white bun with wooden pin, quilted indigo jeogori, grey knit scarf, plum skirt, gomusin shoes). Thug = very chubby greasy gangster (exact face with eyebrow scar, shiny black puffer jacket open, red-black floral silk shirt, gold chain, gold ring, black track pants, dirty white sneakers). Leopard = giant adult snow leopard (exact smoky grey-white coat, black rosettes, pale icy-blue eyes, long thick ringed tail). Hut = snowy thatched mountain tea hut on a hilltop with a small snowy yard, a footpath winding down the slope toward camera, and vast misty snow mountains on the right. Keep every identity, outfit, marking and set identical to refs throughout. Only these three beings exist in the scene.

PROP LOCKS: PROP-STICK = gnarled dark-brown wooden walking stick, always in Grandma's right hand, she leans on it every moment. PROP-BOWL = small round brown earthenware bowl of raw red meat chunks, always in Grandma's left hand, never dropped, never changes.

STAGING LOCK: Grandma stands in the snowy yard just in front of the Hut doorway, facing the footpath/camera, for the entire scene; she never walks away. Thug comes UP the footpath and stops 2 metres in front of her, his BACK to the footpath and slope. Leopard later comes up that SAME footpath from the slope behind Thug. Snow falling softly throughout.

LOOK: overcast winter daylight, cool blue-white snow, warm amber glow from the hut door, low saturation, low-to-mid contrast, filmic grain, soft halation. Ambient wind, crunching snow.

PACING: natural unhurried Korean delivery, short pauses between lines; never rushed.

0–6s: Opening aerial FPV drone shot: high above vast misty snow mountain ranges, gliding smoothly over frosted pine ridges and drifting clouds, then diving gracefully down the slope toward the tiny snow-covered Hut on the hilltop, slowing and pushing in to a medium shot of Grandma standing in the yard, leaning on PROP-STICK, holding PROP-BOWL.

6–11s: Grandma looks around the hills, calling sweetly and caringly, like to her own son:
Grandma (sing-song, tender): 「우리 아가~ 밥 먹자… 어디 갔니, 우리 아들~」
She calls once more, peering at the ridges, smiling.

11–17s: Heavy crunching steps. Thug stomps up the footpath, panting, chin raised, and stops in front of her.
Thug (rough, ruthless): 「할매, 이번 달 자릿세. 빨리 내놔.」
Grandma looks at him calmly and gives a small sly smirk.
Grandma (humble, gentle): 「잠깐만… 우리 고양이 밥 좀 주고. 그다음에 줄게.」

17–22s: Thug scoffs proudly, pinching his thumb and index finger close together in front of his face to mock something tiny.
Thug (angry, sarcastic): 「그 쪼끄만 망할 고양이 기다릴 시간 없어!」
Grandma's eyes shift past his shoulder to the slope; a happy, satisfied smile spreads.
Grandma (warm): 「아이고… 저기 오네.」

22–30s: Thug turns around just as the giant snow leopard lunges from the slope behind him.
```

### Sleeping Watchwoman Slapstick Comedy

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A prompt for a cinematic slapstick comedy scene where a sleeping watchwoman accidentally defeats thieves, requiring consistency with a reference image.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a cinematic, ultra-realistic slapstick comedy scene. The watchwoman’s face and outfit must match the reference image throughout.

Shot 1 (0–3s): At a warehouse entrance, a night watchwoman sleeps deeply in a chair, feet on a crate, snoring with a half-eaten bag of chips on her lap. Five thieves sneak in, smirking at her.

Shot 2 (3–8s): The thieves try passing her, but her unconscious movements effortlessly clothesline one, trip another into crates, and send a third crashing into a shelf. She keeps eating chips and sleepily mutters, “Too slow…”

Shot 3 (8–13s): The remaining thieves nervously huddle before the leader charges her. Without opening her eyes, she elbows him in the stomach, mumbling, “Five more minutes.” He collapses beside the others.

Shot 4 (13–18s): The last thief attempts to escape, but her outstretched leg trips him into boxes. All five thieves lie scattered across the warehouse while she finishes her chips, tosses the empty bag perfectly into a trash can, and uses one thief as a footrest.

Shot 5 (18–23s): Police sirens approach. She briefly opens one eye, surveys the defeated thieves with a deadpan expression, mutters, “Called it,” and immediately falls back asleep. Her drool resumes as she snores.

Shot 6 (23–30s): Police rush in, find the thieves defeated, and stare at the sleeping watchwoman in disbelief. An officer tries waking her, but she lazily swats his hand away. The final close-up reveals a fresh bag of chips mysteriously resting in her lap as she smiles peacefully in her sleep.

Style: Fast-paced slapstick comedy, exaggerated but harmless physical gags, expressive reactions, consistent character appearance, smooth shot transitions, cinematic warehouse lighting, impeccable comedic timing, and a satisfying punchline.
```

### Korean Autumn Romance Animation

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A detailed prompt for a cinematic 3D animated short film about a Korean love story spanning decades, focusing on character design and visual direction.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
MEET ME UNDER THE MAPLE TREE

Duration: 20 seconds | Cinematic 3D Animation | Korean Romance | Autumn Nostalgia | 16:9
ANIMATION STYLE AND VISUAL DIRECTION
Create a stunning, high-quality cinematic 3D animated short film set in South Korea. Use sophisticated character animation, beautifully detailed environments, realistic fabric textures, expressive faces, delicate lighting, and fluid natural movements.
The visual style should combine the charm of a beautifully animated romantic film with the emotional depth and cinematic composition of a nostalgic Korean love story.
Characters should have elegant, attractive facial features, expressive eyes, beautifully animated hair, and subtly stylized proportions while maintaining believable anatomy. Skin, knitted fabric, wood, falling leaves, and lighting should have rich, detailed textures.

Use warm autumn colors, golden sunlight, burgundy-red accents, soft atmospheric depth, delicate depth of field, and subtle film grain.

Important: This is a fully animated 3D film, not live-action. Avoid flat 2D animation, exaggerated cartoon proportions, childish character designs, anime styling, or plastic-looking 3D characters.

CHARACTER DESIGN AND CONTINUITY

THE ELDERLY WOMAN — PRESENT DAY

A graceful, exceptionally beautiful 72-year-old Korean woman with delicate facial features, expressive dark eyes, elegant silver-black hair tied loosely at the back, and naturally detailed age lines.

Her face should communicate kindness, wisdom, warmth, and a lifetime of beautiful memories. Her wrinkles should be delicately rendered rather than exaggerated. Give her a gentle smile and expressive eyes that can communicate happiness and nostalgia without dialogue.

She wears a vintage cream knitted cardigan over a muted-brown dress.

Her home is an old, cozy Korean wooden house featuring traditional wooden windows, faded curtains, aged furniture, a small wooden cabinet, an old analogue clock, and warm afternoon sunlight streaming inside.

On a wooden table sits an old keepsake box containing a faded photograph and a carefully preserved deep-red knitted scarf.

THE YOUNG WOMAN — 1998 FLASHBACK

The exact same character at 23 years old.

She is exceptionally beautiful, with delicate Korean facial features, large expressive dark eyes, smooth skin, long glossy black hair with soft bangs, and a naturally charming smile.

Her character design should feel elegant, romantic, and timeless, with subtle stylization, detailed hair strands, expressive facial animation, and graceful movements.

She wears a vintage cream wool coat, an ivory blouse, a dark pleated skirt, and a beautiful deep-red knitted scarf given to her by her first love.

The scarf must have detailed wool fibers, visible knitted patterns, soft folds, and natural movement.

THE BOYFRIEND — 1998 FLASHBACK

An exceptionally handsome 25-year-old Korean man with expressive dark eyes, a naturally defined jawline, thick black hair styled in a classic late-1990s Korean fashion, and a warm, sincere smile.

He wears a charcoal wool overcoat, a cream knitted sweater, dark trousers, and vintage leather shoes.

His animated expressions should be subtle and believable. His smile should feel shy, affectionate, and genuine.

Both characters must remain visually consistent throughout the flashback, with matching facial features, hairstyles, clothing, and proportions.

SCENE 1 — THE KEEPSAKE

TIME: 0–5 SECONDS

Inside a cozy old Korean wooden house, golden autumn sunlight streams through faded curtains.

Outside the window, red, amber, and golden maple leaves drift gently through the air.

The elderly woman sits beside a wooden table and slowly opens an old wooden keepsake box.

Inside is a faded photograph of her younger self standing beside a handsome young Korean man beneath a maple tree.

A deep-red knitted scarf rests carefully beneath the photograph.

She gently lifts the scarf with both hands. Her fingers softly move across its knitted texture.

A small, affectionate smile appears on her face. Her eyes glisten with a subtle tear as she remembers her first love.

Her expression is peaceful, happy, and filled with gratitude.

CAMERA: Slow cinematic push-in, beginning with a medium shot of the woman before moving into detailed close-ups of her hands, the red scarf, and the faded photograph.

ANIMATION DETAILS: Subtle hand movements, natural blinking, gentle breathing, delicate facial expressions, and softly moving curtains.

LIGHTING: Warm amber sunlight, soft shadows, beautiful highlights on the knitted wool, and realistic light bouncing off the wooden furniture.

SCENE 2 — A HAPPY MEMORY

TIME: 5–10 SECONDS

The camera slowly moves toward the old photograph.

The photograph fills the screen, and its image gradually transforms into a living, animated memory.

Suddenly, we are transported to autumn 1998.

A beautiful young Korean woman stands beneath a magnificent maple tree in a peaceful university garden. Golden leaves float around her as a gentle breeze moves her long black hair.

She wears her cream wool coat and the deep-red knitted scarf.

Her exceptionally handsome boyfriend approaches, carrying a small paper bag from an old-fashioned Korean bakery.

He smiles shyly and gently adjusts the scarf around her neck.

She looks up at him and laughs softly.

He brushes a loose strand of hair away from her face. They exchange a warm, affectionate smile.

Their expressions should communicate the excitement and tenderness of young love.

VISUAL EFFECT: A dreamy, softly glowing atmosphere with delicate floating leaves, gentle sunlight filtering through the maple canopy, subtle depth of field, and beautifully rendered autumn colors.

The memory should feel warm, happy, and precious, as though the elderly woman is reliving her favorite moment.

CAMERA: Gentle tracking movement, intimate close-ups, and graceful transitions between their expressions.

SCENE 3 — THEIR LITTLE WORLD

TIME: 10–15 SECONDS

The young couple walks along an old stone pathway covered in colorful fallen maple leaves.

The young man playfully steps on a dry leaf, making it crackle.

The woman laughs and lightly taps his arm.

He reaches for her hand. Their fingers gently interlock.

She looks at him with a playful smile before resting her head briefly against his shoulder.

They stop beneath the magnificent maple tree.

He gently adjusts the deep-red scarf around her neck and looks into her eyes.

BOYFRIEND — NATURAL KOREAN DIALOGUE:

"너한테 잘 어울려."

ENGLISH SUBTITLE:

"It looks beautiful on you."

The woman smiles shyly, lowers her eyes, and then looks back at him.

GIRL — NATURAL KOREAN DIALOGUE:

"정말?"

ENGLISH SUBTITLE:

"Really?"

He nods and smiles warmly.

She laughs softly.

A few maple leaves float between them as they continue looking at each other.

ANIMATION DETAILS: Fluid body movement, natural eye contact, subtle smiles, gentle hair movement, realistic scarf physics, and leaves responding naturally to the breeze.

The scene should feel intimate, playful, innocent, and genuinely happy.

SCENE 4 — THE MEMORY SHE KEPT

TIME: 15–20 SECONDS

A close-up of the young woman's joyful smile.

The image softly dissolves back into the present day.

The elderly woman sits beside the window in her wooden home, holding the same deep-red scarf against her chest.

She smiles tenderly at the photograph.

Her eyes glisten with nostalgic tears, but she does not look heartbroken. Instead, she looks peaceful and grateful to have experienced such beautiful love.

She carefully places the scarf beside the photograph and gently smooths its folded edge.

Outside the window, a golden maple leaf floats past.

She looks
```

```
Shot 3 (8–17s): A worker in a yellow high-visibility vest, black cap and grey work pants pushes a red-and-white ride-on floor grinder with a black vacuum hose, polishing the hardened epoxy in smooth passes; golden key shapes emerge embedded in the glossy floor like terrazzo, reflections of the window and the worker visible on the mirror-like surface.

Shot 4 (17–23s): Two workers in black polo shirts and black caps assemble a dark walnut bed frame in the center of the room, place a white mattress, smooth out white bedding and pillows, then wooden nightstands with white ceramic lamps appear on both sides; the finished bed with beige accent pillows stands on the polished floor full of golden keys as the workers walk out of frame.

Bright natural daylight, soft shadows, satisfying ASMR renovation timelapse mood, photorealistic textures, smooth motion, no text, no watermark.
```

### Helicopter Trampoline Jump Video Prompt

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

> A detailed prompt for generating a 15-second realistic video of a person jumping from a helicopter onto a trampoline on the sea and bouncing back up, specifically designed for Seedance 2.0.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
[Priority Reference Image & Name Fixing]
@ image1 = Description Name: Protagonist
The protagonist's identity, face, hairstyle, body type, costume, accessories, equipment, and shoe appearance standards.
Use only the names fixed above in the subsequent text.
15 seconds, 3:4 aspect ratio, photorealistic live-action, set over a bright blue sea during the day, continuous audio video. No scene cuts throughout the entire duration. Use character reference only for the protagonist's appearance; do not incorporate three-view diagrams, parallel displays, or text/sample layouts into the video. Maintain character identity, costumes, accessories, and physique throughout. No BGM, subtitles, captions, or logos.
Characters: Protagonist, Crew A, Crew B, Crew C (4 total). A medium-sized civilian filming helicopter with a white fuselage and blue lines. Black skid landing gear, open wide side sliding door, external steps for boarding, dark gray interior. The helicopter maintains a constant altitude above the sea surface from start to finish. A coastline is visible in the distance, and a circular trampoline is placed on the sea surface far below. The protagonist stands on the external step outside the open door, looking at the distant trampoline below. Crew A stands near the door wearing a blue windbreaker, black life vest, and aviation communication headset. Crew B stands inside the aircraft near the door wearing an orange windbreaker, black life vest, and aviation communication headset. Crew C is deep inside the aircraft wearing a green windbreaker, black life vest, and aviation communication headset. All three crew members' faces are visible. The camera starts from the same filming position near the helicopter door, capturing the protagonist, three crew members, and the sea beyond the door with a slightly wide-angle lens. Do not change the camera's installation position; adjust its vertical orientation according to the fall and ascent. Wind noise and helicopter rotor sounds continue naturally throughout.
0.0–3.0s: Crew A looks at the protagonist and smiles, asking "Ready?" The protagonist turns their face to Crew A, nods, then looks directly into the camera lens, showing tension and excitement mixed with a smile, saying energetically in natural Japanese, "Okay, I'm going!" The visible mouth movements of both parties match their respective full dialogue. Crew B raises a fist lightly while smiling, and Crew C smiles happily, leaning out slightly and nodding. After speaking, the protagonist takes a deep breath, looks down at the circular trampoline, kicks off the external step, and jumps out.
3.0–6.2s: The moment the protagonist jumps out of the helicopter, Crew B shouts towards them, "Go!" The camera quickly turns downward while maintaining the filming position near the door, tracking the falling protagonist in the center of the frame. The protagonist extends their body as they fall, becoming smaller in the frame as the distance increases. The circular trampoline below becomes visible, and the protagonist approaches it aiming for the center. While the camera is pointed down, the three crew members are off-screen, but cheerful cheers can be heard from inside the aircraft. Rotor sounds and wind noises continue.
6.2–7.7s: The protagonist lands near the center of the circular trampoline on the sea surface. The stretched jumping surface sinks significantly under the protagonist's weight, then strongly pushes back immediately after the descent stops. The camera remains pointing down, showing the landing, the sinking of the jumping surface, and the reversal to upward momentum due to rebound in one continuous shot. Impact sounds of landing and rebound are audible.
7.7–11.6s: The protagonist bounces high towards the helicopter with the momentum of the rebound, pulling knees to chest to fold body small, spinning vigorously in mid-air. The camera adjusts upwards to match the rising protagonist from the same filming position. As the protagonist gets closer, they become larger in the frame, and the open door and external step become visible again. The protagonist rises rapidly all the way to just before the helicopter's external step, quickly unwinding the spin and extending legs at the moment of reaching the step, planting both feet firmly on the same external step they jumped from without hesitation. Upon landing, absorb shock with knees and immediately straighten upper body, returning to the same position, body orientation, and standing posture as at the beginning. Film the approach to the helicopter, unwinding of rotation, planting of both feet, and return to posture as a sharp, quick series of movements continuing seamlessly at normal speed. The helicopter's altitude above the sea surface does not change.
11.6–15.0s: The camera returns to the initial orientation around the door. Crew A claps smilingly towards the protagonist. Crew B joyfully thrusts a fist up and shouts briefly, "Yay--!" Crew C looks at the protagonist, smiles, and claps hands. The protagonist, breathing heavily, turns face to camera lens, eyes shining, speaking brightly and naturally with excitement and joy of safe return: "Oh wow! Oh wow, that was awesome!" Match mouth movements of Crew B and protagonist with their respective full dialogue during speech. Final frame: Protagonist standing on the same initial external step, finishing talking to camera with excited expression, behind them Crew A and Crew C are clapping, and Crew B is rejoicing. End at 15 seconds with crew clapping and rotor sounds continuing.
```

### Seedance 2.0 Viral Crocodile Attack Video Prompt

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)

> A detailed prompt for generating a realistic, handheld-camera style video of a crocodile attack at a zoo using Seedance 2.0.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
[Video Specifications and Overall Style]

9:16 vertical screen, 15 seconds, single continuous shot. A thrilling scene accidentally captured by a tourist with an ordinary phone inside a Chinese wildlife park. Initially calm, the photographer reacts half a beat late to a giant crocodile's ambush, causing camera shake, misalignment, and hurried re-framing of the subject. Retain the authenticity of amateur mobile footage.

[Character and Reference Locking]

The female protagonist uses preset adult Chinese female character #1 @598c0e35-7c27-4b7c-8bac-19bb206ff1c3, wearing preset outfit #2 @d57ec59a-c0a5-4eae-84f4-4f02ef21a92d, strictly maintaining the facial features, long hair, clothing style, color, and material from the references. #1 is the person, #2 is the outfit; numbers do not appear in the frame.

The administrator is a Chinese male around forty years old, short black hair, wearing khaki short-sleeved work clothes, dark trousers, with a work badge on his chest, standing behind and to the side of the female protagonist.

There are a few adult Chinese tourists in the background, wearing summer casual wear, naturally scattered along the boardwalk.

[Scene and Camera Position]

Tropical crocodile viewing area, old wooden boardwalk extending along a deep green pool, opposite bank lined with bamboo, palms, and shrubs. Thick wooden railing extends diagonally from bottom right to background.

External photographer stands about one meter behind and to the side of the female protagonist, slightly higher than her hand-held phone position, shooting vertically downwards. The right side of the frame shows the female protagonist, administrator, and boardwalk; left and bottom show the pool.

Humid daytime, natural sunlight filtering through vegetation, slight green reflection on water surface. Ordinary phone 1x main camera, slight hand shake, tilt, compression artifacts, noise in shadows, brief blur during rapid movement. No cinematic grading, drone shots, or portrait bokeh.

[Female Protagonist's Phone Orientation - Must Be Accurate]

The female protagonist is using the rear camera to film the crocodile in the water, not taking a selfie, nor showing the phone to the external photographer.

She holds the phone horizontally with both hands at the ends, fingers avoiding the rear camera. The phone is below her chest, near the outer edge of the railing, clearly tilted downwards, with the rear lens direction accurately aimed at the huge shadow underwater in the lower left.

Phone screen faces up, slightly towards her face; back and rear lens face down towards water. She looks down at the screen, observing the target in water through it.

From the external photographer's slightly higher side-rear angle, mainly see the phone screen and side edges, should not clearly see the entire phone back and camera module facing the external photographer.

Screen content matches real water surface: same water patch and blurry shadow visible, no selfie face, irrelevant scenery, or wrong direction.

When adjusting wrists slightly, rear lens always follows underwater shadow. Only when crocodile attacks does she pull back phone in fear, disrupting orientation.

[Crocodile and First Frame State]

Only one abnormally large dark gray-black crocodile. When leaping, head size approaches upper body of an adult, open mouth occupies nearly half left frame, size reference original video, no small crocodile generated.

First frame: crocodile fully submerged, only huge blurry long shadow like sunken wood. Water reflections and turbidity hide details, cannot clearly identify head, eyes, nostrils, scales, teeth, nothing exposed above water.

Crocodile maintains same volume throughout, no sudden expansion during leap.

[0-2s: Filming Underwater Shadow]

Female protagonist stands inside railing, leaning forward slightly, holding phone horizontally, aiming rear lens down at huge shadow in lower left water.

She looks down at screen facing her, shadow in screen corresponds to shadow in pool. External photographer captures phone screen, her profile, and water below from side-rear.

Administrator seriously warns:

"Ma'am, step back, don't lean on the railing!"

Water has only slight ripples, crocodile still hidden.

[2-4s: Target Approaches, Phone Follows]

Huge underwater outline slowly approaches female protagonist's position. She adjusts wrist slightly, keeping rear lens aimed at moving shadow, screen facing her, not turning phone back to photographer.

Administrator steps closer, reaching out to support her, tone urgent:

"That's not wood, it's alive! Don't lean in!"

She starts pulling body back but phone still filming water. End of segment: crocodile snout and eyes just breaking water surface.

[4-6s: Giant Crocodile Ambush]

Crocodile violently bursts water surface, huge head rises along outside of railing, open mouth instantly occupies nearly half left frame, splashing large amount of water.

Female protagonist screams, hurriedly pulls back phone and arm. Phone orientation loses stability due to shock, must have been correctly aimed before.

Crocodile briefly bites into her forearm extended outside railing and nearby fabric. Administrator immediately supports her upper arm and shoulder/back, stabilizing her center of gravity inside railing.

External photographer retreats half step in shock, camera briefly shakes upward, no cuts or wound close-ups.

[6-9s: Escape and Fall]

Crocodile briefly releases grip, head drops back, female protagonist seizes chance to retract injured forearm, administrator pulls her to inner boardwalk. Action must have clear release, arm cannot pass through closed teeth.

Phone slips from hand, flips once, falls into pool.

Crocodile slams back into water, white splash. Female protagonist covers forearm, small amount of red liquid seeps from injury, forming short thin streaks, staining fabric at contact point, one or two drops falling onto wooden boardwalk.

No deep wounds shown, no spraying, no heavy bleeding.

Administrator shouts:

"Step back! Don't reach out! Don't pick up phone!"

[9-11s: Tourist Panic]

Female protagonist retreats to middle of boardwalk, protecting injured forearm, shoulders tense, breathing rapid.

Nearby tourists first shrink back, grab companions; those further away turn upon hearing scream, some put away phones, others make way, exclaiming sequentially:

"Step back quickly!"

"Don't lean on railing!"

Crowd reaction has time lag, not synchronized, no clipping, no falling into pool. Crocodile remains underwater, no second attack.

[11-13s: Administrator Evacuation]

Administrator stands between female protagonist and railing, continuing to protect her retreat, gesturing for tourists to make way:

"Back up! Everyone don't crowd!"

A nearby tourist offers tissue, female protagonist accepts and presses same injury spot, tissue gradually stains red.

Photographer retreats slightly and corrects composition, capturing female protagonist, administrator, and panicking retreating tourists.

[13-15s: Aftermath]

Female protagonist covers forearm, gasping, trembling voice says:

"I'm scared... it really bit me!"

Administrator still protects her, warily watching pool. Background tourists whisper warnings:

"Stay away, stop filming!"

Ends with female protagonist shaken, crowd retreating, water ripples subsiding.

[Live Audio]

All live audio from phone, Chinese dialogue, no music, narration, subtitles.

Start includes low tourist chatter, bamboo leaf rustling, slight water sounds. Administrator voice near and clear, shifting from serious warning to urgent restraint as danger occurs.

During crocodile emergence: heavy water sound, female scream, nearby gasps; followed by phone splashdown, water crash, rapid panting, chaotic footsteps. Max splash and scream cause mic clipping briefly, then auto-gain recovery.

Distant tourist voices more scattered, lighter, no monster roars added.

[Continuity and Key Constraints]

Opening phone must correctly film underwater target: screen up facing protagonist, rear lens down aimed at crocodile; external photographer sees mainly screen/sides. Prohibit rear lens facing photographer while depicting filming underwater target.

First frame only blurry huge underwater shadow, no clear crocodile head/eyes/scales/teeth. Red liquid only after bite.

Maintain continuity of Character #1, Outfit #2, Administrator, Crocodile, Boardwalk. Injury always same forearm, red streaks/fabric damage/tissue press location correspond.

Only one phone, does not return to hand after fall. Protagonist/Administrator always inside railing, Crocodile @d57ec59a-c0a5-4eae-84f4-4f02ef21a92d always in pool. Maintain causal relationships of contact, weight, bite, release, retreat actions throughout.

No wound close-ups, dismemberment, deep tissue display. Single continuous shot, no slow motion, replays, studio reveals, UI overlays, watermarks.
```

```
A colossal whale made of folded origami paper with crisp creases. A paper cut village of rooftops and windmills with tiny paper villagers flying kites.
SHOT 1 (0 to 3s): Wide shot matching the reference image, the whale glides forward and its folded fins unfold and refold with crisp creasing motions, paper clouds sliding past in parallax layers.
SHOT 2 (3 to 6s): Medium shot, 50mm lens, villagers on a hill tug their kite strings as the whale's shadow passes over them, windmill blades spinning faster in its wake.
SHOT 3 (6 to 9s): Close-up, 85mm lens, the whale's eye, a small cut circle, blinks as backlight glows through the thin paper of its head, revealing faint printed text inside the folds.
SHOT 4 (9 to 12s): Low angle tracking shot, 24mm lens, the whale dives toward the village and its tail sweeps a wave of paper confetti and paper birds out across the rooftops.
SHOT 5 (12 to 15s): Extreme wide shot, 16mm lens, the whale rises into the sky and unfolds completely into a flat sheet that becomes a giant paper sun, lighting the whole village warmly.
Rustling paper, soft crinkling folds, windmill creaks, a gentle whale call that sounds like a breath through paper, children laughing, music box and soft piano.
```

### Pink Supercar Transformation Action

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A prompt for a surreal action sequence where a toy pink supercar transforms into a real one, leading to a drift through Tokyo streets.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Ultra-realistic cinematic action sequence featuring a young woman with long black hair wearing a black oversized graphic T-shirt and black boots, sitting outside a suburban house holding a miniature pink supercar. Suddenly, the toy car magically transforms into a full-size, ultra-realistic pink supercar and launches into the air, flying through a quiet residential street before transitioning into a dramatic Tokyo-style urban intersection. The woman appears inside the driver's seat as the pink supercar drifts aggressively through the city, spinning across the crosswalk with realistic tire smoke, spinning wheels, cinematic motion blur, and reflections on the asphalt. The car accelerates through the intersection, leaving thick white smoke trails, then slides dramatically toward a brightly illuminated Japanese convenience store. Cut to a wide cinematic shot of the car drifting outside the store as pedestrians react in surprise. Final scene: the woman walks confidently out of the convenience store entrance, surrounded by scattered groceries and glass reflections, while the pink supercar remains visible in the background.
```

### Zoo Lion Attack Cinematic Video

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A prompt for generating a tense, realistic video of a woman visiting a zoo who suddenly encounters a charging lion.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a 20-second cinematic, ultra-realistic AI video featuring a young Korean woman with long, dark, slightly wavy hair, wearing a white blouse and dark pants. Begin with a close-up of her sitting beside a window, looking thoughtfully at her smartphone in soft natural daylight. Transition to an outdoor zoo setting where she stands near a railing, casually using her phone while observing the surroundings. Show her approaching the lion enclosure and raising her smartphone to capture a photo of a majestic lion below. Suddenly, the lion charges toward her, creating a tense and unexpected moment. She drops her phone and struggles to escape as the lion lunges at her inside the enclosure. Capture her frightened expressions, realistic body movements, flying dust, and the lion's powerful motion. Use dynamic camera angles, smooth cinematic transitions, natural sunlight, realistic shadows, and detailed environmental textures. Maintain consistent facial features, hairstyle, clothing, and body proportions throughout the video, with dramatic pacing and photorealistic quality.
```
